- **surprisal**: Use when reasoning about the information of one event — surprisal I(p) = −log₂(p) bits. A certain event (p=1) carries zero bits; a rare event carries many. It is the bit-cost of a specific outcome, and in erpax the bit-cost of a specific forgery: forging a thing of probability p under the wired graph costs −log₂(p) bits. Additive for independent events (I(p·q)=I(p)+I(q)), so improbabilities stack like uuid-chained dimensions; expected surprisal is Shannon entropy. ([erpax/erpax](https://github.com/erpax/erpax/tree/HEAD/src/surprisal) · ★2)

- **survival-shelter-water-fire-and-food**: Use for the core needs in their actual order of urgency: thermoregulation and shelter including how fast hypothermia develops and what insulation and wind actually do, water with the real timelines, treatment options and the risks of untreated sources, fire and reliable ignition, and food — why it comes last and how long you genuinely have without it.
